The Nazareth - Church will accept donations of cash or publicly traded securities. Gifts of in-kind services will be accepted at the discretion of the Nazareth - Church.

Certain other gifts, real property, personal property, in-kind gifts, non-liquid securities, and contributions whose sources are not transparent or whose use is restricted in some manner, must be reviewed prior to acceptance due to the special obligations raised or liabilities they may pose for Nazareth - Church.

The Nazareth - Church will provide acknowledgments to donors meeting tax requirements for property received by the charity as a gift. The Nazareth - Church will respect the intent of the donor relating to gifts for restricted purposes and those relating to the desire to remain anonymous. With respect to anonymous gifts, the Nazareth - Church will restrict information about the donor to only those staff members with a need to know.
